ParlaMint-UA_2010-07-10-m0.u478	Dear Adam Ivanovich, distinguished Volodymyr Mikhailovich, distinguished colleagues! At the request of the Supreme Council of Ukraine, the Committee on State Building and Local Self-Government, at its meeting on July 9, 2010, considered observations and suggestions from the legislators of the legislative initiative to the draft Ukrainian Law on Elections of Deputies of the Supreme Council of the Autonomous Republic of Crimea, Local Councils and Rural, Rural, Urban Heads, adopted as a result of the first reading on the basis of July 1, 2010. [[Registration number 6601]] A comparative table for a bill containing 1,336 amendments and suggestions from law - abiding entities has been prepared by the Committee for Second Reading. As a result of the consideration of amendments and proposals at the 109th meeting of the Committee, 7 were taken into account in part and 1229 were rejected. During the consideration of the draft, the members of the committee stressed that part of the second article 66, adopted on the basis of the bill, provided that the purchase of goods, wages, services for preparing and holding alternate, auxiliary and first local elections was carried out by the Central Electoral Commission at the expense of the State budget of Ukraine, by the territorial election commissions, at the expense of the relevant local budget, which was awarded as a targeted subsidy from the State budget of Ukraine, and by the re- and intermediate local elections, at the expense of local budgets without applying tender competition procedures in the order established by the Cabinet of Ministers of Ukraine. At the committee meeting, it was noted that the establishment or modification of the terms and conditions of procedure, the purchase of goods, jobs and services at the expense of public funds was governed by the Ukrainian Law on State Procurement, which was adopted by Parliament on June 1, 2010, and became effective on June 30, 2010. The law also extends to cases that are subject to the purchase of goods, jobs, and services for the preparation and conduct of elections and referendums. This part of article 2 of the Law provides for cases where tender competition procedures are not applied. But this article does not provide that the purchase of goods, jobs, and services for the preparation and conduct of elections and referendums is carried out with specificities. In view of the foregoing, the committee considers it appropriate to amend part 3 of article 2 of the Ukrainian Law on State Procurement. After hearing the information of the members of the Central Electoral Commission, representatives of the Ministry of Justice, having found out the position of Ukrainian national deputies, members of the committee and on the basis of questions, the Committee recommends to the Supreme Council of Ukraine, in accordance with article 123, part 1, paragraph 3, of the Supreme Council of Ukraine’s Rules of Procedure, a draft of the Ukrainian Law on Elections of Deputies of the Supreme Council of the Autonomous Republic of Crimea, Local Councils and Rural, Rural, Urban Heads, registration number.
ParlaMint-UA_2010-07-10-m0.u479	Finish up, please.
ParlaMint-UA_2010-07-10-m0.u480	As a result, consider the second reading, the second reading, and the whole. And in the final decision of the bill to apply the rules of article 50 of the Supreme Council of Ukraine's Rules of Procedure on a one-time deviation from procedures and to make further changes to the draft law, namely: to supplement section 14 of the draft Law on the Election of Deputies of the Supreme Council of the Autonomous Republic of the Crimea with the following content: “Participation of the third article 2 of the Ukrainian Law on State Procurement since 1.06.2010 shall be supplemented by a paragraph of the following content: “Products, works and services which are purchased for the preparation and conduct of elections and referendums in Ukraine”. Dear colleagues, according to the changes that you and I have made to the calendar plan for the sixth session of the Supreme Council of Ukraine on July 10th for the completion of the plenary at the sixth session of the Supreme Council of Ukraine. Dear colleagues, in fact in 50 days of plenary work, held 71 mornings and evening plenary meetings. Three parliamentary hearings were held with the appropriate decisions of 761. In order to carry out the main tasks of the session, we have carried out this work with you in particular, we have assigned a new composition to the Cabinet of Ministers of Ukraine, we have approved the State Budget and the State Economic and Social Development Programme for 2010. The principles of domestic and foreign policy, the laws on public procurement, the judiciary and the status of judges, have been updated by the Code of Budgets of Ukraine. Of the 23 bills designated by the President of Ukraine as urgent, ten were adopted as a whole as laws of Ukraine, four more adopted as a basis for their preparation for the second reading. In total, a decision was taken in the course of the session on 1031 drafts, including a total of 133 laws, 84 drafts in the first reading, 814 recasts, rejected and withdrawn from consideration of 365 in accordance with the appeal procedure. This indicates that the Supreme Council of Ukraine has become more involved in legislation, including its own so - called production. And also, your honors, during this period we have adopted 500 organizational and procedural decrees. The section* of the legal entities of the legislative initiative discussed 27 of the bills submitted by the President of Ukraine, 306 by the Cabinet of Ministers of Ukraine, 698 by the Ukrainian National Deputies. The issue of economic policy, sectoral development and legal issues was the subject matter. Of the laws adopted at the end of the session, 73 have been signed and introduced, of which six have been ratified by international treaties and treaties, 57 have been introduced in the new or updated edition of eight laws. Another 54 adopted laws are being prepared or are being signed by the President of Ukraine. In addition, six bills, six laws, were returned by the President of Ukraine for their re-examination. Dear colleagues! At the end of the session, there are 1,273 drafts of the Committees of the Supreme Council of Ukraine, 84 of which are being prepared for the second or subsequent readings.
